# Structural Language Audit

Date: 2026-06-07

Audit scope: tracked text files only. PDF filenames and references were inspected as text where present; PDF binaries were not opened or edited.

Occurrence count: 1786

Classification counts:

| Classification | Count |
| --- | ---: |
| precise technical use | 597 |
| acceptable plain-language use | 78 |
| repeated but acceptable | 34 |
| vague abstract use | 55 |
| historical archive language | 5 |
| quotation or title | 181 |
| identifier, slug, URL, or compatibility reference | 548 |
| unclear | 288 |


## Method

Searched tracked text files case-insensitively for whole-word forms: `structural`, `structurally`, `structure`, and `structures`. The audit classifies usage only. It does not recommend route changes or public copy edits in this pass.

Tracked filenames were also inspected. Filename-only hits are compatibility references and were not counted as prose occurrences:

- `assets/papers/why-structural-dependence-hides-behind-functional-success.pdf`
- `assets/why-structural-dependence-hides-behind-functional-success.pdf`
- `content/essays/exodus-and-freedom-without-inner-structure.md`
